- **notary**: Use when a fact must be given public faith — recorded in a bound chronological register, timestamped, sealed tamper-evident, and made presumptively authentic. The legal notarial act modelled on erpax primitives: the seal is a content-uuid, the protocol is an append-only hash-chain, authenticity is an inclusion proof; the honest boundary is that real legal force needs a commissioned notary or an eIDAS/RFC-3161 trust service.
